Binary local search details:
We have h in {0,1}, length n even, n/2 ones. Objective count T = max_{L=1..n-1} max(A_L,B_L). C=2T/n (C0=0). For n fixed, minimizing T integer. We can maintain arrays A[1..n-1], B. A_L = #i=0..n-L-1 h[i]=0,h[i+L]=1. B_L = #h[i]=1,h[i+L]=0.
Flip one bit at pos p from 1 to 0 changes counts for all L:
- For L such that pair (p-L,p) valid: if h[p-L]=? B? Let's derive in terms h.
Let val old v (0/1). For L=1..n-1.
A_L: pairs (i, i+L). If p is second index i+L => i=p-L≥0; old contribution if h[p-L]=0 and v=1 for h[p]. After flip to 1-v, contribution changes: old = (h[p-L]==0 and v==1), new = (h[p-L]==0 and (1-v)==1) if v? For v=1 to 0: old 1 if h[p-L]=0; new 0 => A_L -= h[p-L]? Wait h=0 indicator. If v=1, old A=1 if left=0. New 0. So delta A = - (1-h[p-L])? Actually if v=1: delta = - (left==0) = -(1-left). For v=0 to 1: old 0, new= (left==0)? delta = left==0. So generally delta A when flip v to 1-v = (1-v)*(1-h[p-L]) - v*(1-h[p-L])? = (1-2v)*(1-h[p-L]).
If p is first index i=p, i+L=p+L<n: old contribution A = (v==0 and h[p+L]==1). New = ((1-v)==0 and right==1) = (v==1 and right==1). Delta = (1-v)*right - v*right = (1-2v)*h[p+L]. If both? For a given L, p can be first or second or both? p first for L≤n-1-p; p second for L≤p. If L both, p is first and second in two different pair? Actually pair index i such that i=p (first) or i=p-L (second). Both only if L=0. For L>0 distinct. So delta_A[L] = (1-2v)*[ (1-h[p-L]) if L≤p + h[p+L] if L≤n-1-p ].
Similarly B_L pairs (i,i+L) h_i=1,h_{i+L}=0. If p first: old v* (1-right), new (1-v)*(1-right) => delta = (1-2v)*(1-h[p+L]). If p second: old left*(1-v), new left*(v)? Wait p second value v. Old B if h[p-L]=1 and v=0; new if left=1 and (1-v)=0 => left=1 and v=1. Delta = v*(1-v?) let's formula: old=left*(1-v), new=left*v, delta=(2v-1)*left. Express symmetric = -(1-2v)*left = (v-1/2)*left. For A second delta=(1-2v)*(1-left). B second delta=(v-0.5)*left? Let's use `s = 1 - 2v` (if old v=0, s=1; if old v=1, s=-1). Then delta_A:
- p first: s * h[right]
- p second: s * (1 - h[left])
Delta_B:
- p first: s * (1 - h[right]) ? old v*(1-right), new (1-v)*(1-right) difference = (1-v - v)*(1-right)= (1-2v)(1-right)=s*(1-right). yes.
- p second: old left*(1-v), new left*v difference = (v - (1-v))left = (2v-1)left = -s*left.
Check v=1, s=-1: flip 1->0. p first: A old v*right = right, new 0 => delta -right? s*right=-right yes. B old 1*(1-right), new 0 => delta -(1-right); s*(1-right)=-(1-right). p second: A old 0, new 1 => delta - (1-left); s*(1-left)=-(1-left). B old left*1? if v=1 old 0? Wait if v=1, B second old left*0=0, new left*1=left; delta +left. -s*left=+left. good.
Thus flipping p changes A[L], B[L] for all L up to max(p,n-1-p). We can vectorize: create arrays `deltaA`, `deltaB` length n. Could precompute left/right indicator? For each flip, need arrays of h shifted.
For p, v:
s = 1 - 2*v.
Can compute `cols`? Let's create arrays length n (index L 0..n-1). Initialize zeros. For L where p is first (L=1..n-1-p): deltaA[L] += s*h[p+L]; deltaB[L]+=s*(1-h[p+L]). For L second (L=1..p): deltaA[L]+= s*(1-h[p-L]); deltaB[L]-= s*h[p-L]. Add to A[:L]. Use slices:
- r = h[p+1:] (length n-p-1) for first L 1..n-p-1: A[1:n-p] += s*r; B[1:n-p] += s*(1-r)
- l = h[:p] (length p) for second L 1..p reversed? For L=1..p, left=h[p-L]. The array h[p-1], h[p-2],...,h[0] corresponds L=1..p. Need slice `left_arr = h[p-1::-1]` view maybe. A[1:p+1] += s*(1-left_arr); B[1:p+1] -= s*left_arr.

Pseudo:
```
def apply_flip(h, p, A, B, n):
    v = int(h[p])
    s = 1 - 2*v
    # first: L=1..n-1-p, right = h[p+1:]
    m1 = n - p - 1
    if m1 > 0:
        r = h[p+1:p+n]  # length m1
        # r int8
        A[1:m1+1] = A[1:m1+1].astype? + s*r? dtype int32. But A slice is int32; `A[1:m1+1] += s * r.astype(np.int32)`
        B[1:m1+1] += s * (1 - r.astype(np.int32))
    m2 = p
    if m2 > 0:
        # left reversed length p: h[p-1],...,h[0]
        lrev = h[p-1::-1].astype(np.int32)
        A[1:m2+1] += s * (1 - lrev)
        B[1:m2+1] -= s * lrev
    h[p] = 1 - v
    return s
```
Check dtype: if h int8, `0-r` yields int8? but okay; use `r32 = h[p+1:].astype(np.int32, copy=False)` but astype copy maybe costly. Could use view by making h int32 from start. h int32 length 512, fine. Then operations easier. Use `h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)`. Then no astype. But memory small. Use int16? int32.
`A` int32. `s*r` where s int, r int32 yields int64? Numpy may upcast to int64, assignment to int32 okay but creates temp. To reduce, use int16? Counts up to 512 fit int16, sums delta small. Use int32 safe.

```
Better branch on s:
If old v=0 (s=1): flip to 1.
- first right r: A += r (0->1 with right=1), B += 1-r.
- second left l: A += 1-l, B -= l.
If old v=1 (s=-1): flip to 0.
- first: A -= r, B -= 1-r.
- second: A -= (1-l), B += l.
This avoids multiplication. We'll write conditions outside to reduce.
```

   if m1: A[1:m1+1] += h[p+1:p+n1]; B[1:m1+1] += 1 - h[...] (temp)

Specifically, let h_i = b_i + ε_i, with b∈{0,1}, ∑ε=0, bounds ε_i∈[-b_i,1-b_i]. Objective for lag L:
F_L = ∑_{i=0}^{m-1} (b_{i+L}+ε_{i+L})(1 - b_i - ε_i) over valid.
= B_L + ∑ [ (1-b_i)ε_{i+L} - b_{i+L}ε_i - ε_{i+L} ε_i ].
Linear part Lparts. For small ε, ignore quadratic. We can minimize max of F_L with linear objective and box constraints: LP! This can find descent direction. Iteratively update. Need top-level `project_box_mean(h,n)` for continuous. Implement water-filling:
```
def project_box_mean(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2
   # adjust by uniform until sum target, if not hit bounds
   for _ in range(10):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
      free=(h>0)&(h<1)
      if not free.any():
         # adjust lowest bound? Should not if target feasible
      h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # final tiny adjust on least near bounds
   diff=target-h.sum(); if diff: idx=argmin(max(h,1-h)); h[idx]+=diff
   return h
```
Uniform projection not exact for box but iterations enough. Could implement water filling: Sort h pieces; but uniform with clipping and repeat converges.

Implement gradient for continuous efficiently:
```
def cont_grad_from_weights(weights, h, n):
    dx=2/n
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    # weights length 2n-1, lag index offset n-1
    # loop L from -n+1 to n-1
    for L in range(-n+1,n):
       w=weights[n-1+L]
       if w<1e-14: continue
       if L==0:
          grad += w*(1-2h)
       elif L>0:
          # derivative for corr offset L: A_L = sum h[i+L](1-h[i])
          # previous: grad[L:] += w*(1 - h[:n-L]); grad[:n-L] -= w*h[L:]
          grad[L:] += w*(1.0 - h[:-L])? need align lengths
          # For i=0..n-L-1, term h[i+L]*(1-h[i]). derivative wrt h[j]:
          # j>=L: wrt h[j] as first: (1 - h[j-L]) for j= L..n-1 -> grad[L:] += w*(1 - h[:n-L])
          # j<n-L: wrt h[j] as second: -h[j+L] -> grad[:n-L] -= w*h[L:]
          # This matches previous.
       else:
          Lp=-L
          # corr offset -L = B_L? derivative: grad[:n-Lp] += w*(1 - h[Lp:]); grad[Lp:] -= w*h[:n-Lp]
```
Use `[:n-L]` views. Need be careful `[:-L]` for L int positive length n-L. Good.

```
def binary_local_search(h0, n, rng, deadline, start_time=None):
    h = resample_to_binary(h0,n) maybe already int
    A,B = init_counts(h,n)
    T,c1,c2,cost = eval_state(A,B)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_T=T; best_c5=2*T/n
    temp=300.0; min_temp=1.0; decay=0.99995
    stall=0; it=0; improve_streak? 
    while time.time() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # choose move
       mode=rng.random()
       if mode < 0.55:
          p1,p0 = choose_target_move(...)
       else:
          p1,p0 = None
       if p1 is None: p1,p0 = random_pair
       if p1 is None: continue
       apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
       apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
       Tn,c1n,c2n,costn = eval_state(A,B)
       diff = costn - cost
       accept = False
       if diff <= -1e-12:
          accept=True
       elif abs(diff) < 1e-12:
          accept = rng.random() < 0.25  # explore plateaus
       else:
          # if diff < 0? done. negative no
          if diff < temp*100? Compute prob safe.
             prob = math.exp(-diff/temp) if diff/temp < 700 else 0
             accept = rng.random() < prob
       if accept:
          h,A,B,T,c1,c2,cost updated (need state variables reassign)
          if costn < best_cost? update best_h copy
          stall = 0 if diff<0 else stall+1? For acceptance, if not decrease, stall++.
       else:
          # undo
          apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
          apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
          stall += 1
       # temperature decay every N? if it%10==0: temp=max(min,temp*decay)
       if it % 10 == 0:
           temp = max(min_temp, temp * decay)
           if stall > 5000:
               # reset temp
               temp = max(temp, 100.0)
               # maybe perturb h by random flips and reevaluate (as uphill)
               # We can do a controlled perturbation: copy state, apply 5 flips, if T not too worse keep
               for _ in range(3):
                   oldh=h.copy(); oldA=A.copy(); oldB=B.copy(); oldT=T; oldcost=cost
                   for _ in range(3): random pair flip
                   Tpc,...=eval
                   if Tpc <= oldT + 3: accept perturbation
                   else restore
               stall=0
       # check best
       if T < best_T or (T==best_T and cost < best_cost): update
```

Let's write top-level functions:
```
def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = beta * (corr - m)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    tot = exps.sum()
    val = m + np.log(tot)/beta
    weights = exps/tot
    return val, weights
```
`corr` full length 2n-1.
Gradient loop: Use weights. Could define:
```
def cont_gradient(weights, h, n, beta? no):
    dx=2.0/n
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    off=n-1
    # Iterate over L from -(n-1) to n-1
    for L in range(-(n-1), n):
       w = float(weights[off+L])
       if w <= 1e-13: continue
       if L == 0:
          grad += w * (1.0 - 2.0*h)
       elif L > 0:
          m = n - L
          grad[L:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
          grad[:m] -= w * h[L:]
       else:
          Lp = -L
          m = n - Lp
          grad[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[Lp:])
          grad[Lp:] -= w * h[:m]
    return dx * grad
```
For L negative, check formula. Let's test with previous grad. For L<0 offset -Lp, corr = sum h[i]*(1-h[i+Lp]) = B_Lp. derivative: term i=0..m-1 h_i(1-h_{i+Lp}). wrt h[j]: if j<m as first: (1 - h[j+Lp]) -> grad[:m] += w*(1 - h[Lp:]); if j>=Lp as second: -h[j-Lp] -> grad[Lp:] -= w*h[:m]. Correct.
Mean constraint: Project after Adam update. But gradient of projection? Not needed. However if we penalize mean, no need. We'll project and then maybe adjust.
Initialize `h` feasible. Use `project_box_mean`.

Adam optimizer:
```
def adam_optimize(h0,n,beta,max_iter,deadline,lr=0.02,rng=None):
   h = project_box_mean(np.asarray(h0,float),n)
   m1=np.zeros(n); v1=np.zeros(n); t=0
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5_from_h(h)
   for it in range(max_iter):
      corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
      sm, weights = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta)
      g=cont_gradient(weights,h,n)
      # add small noise? optional
      t+=1
      m1=beta1*m1+(1-beta1)*g
      v1=beta2*v1+(1-beta2)*g*g
      mhat=m1/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v1/(1-beta2**t)
      h_new = h - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      h_new = project_box_mean(h_new,n)
      c=compute_c5_from_h(h_new) maybe expensive exact each iter; use smooth for tracking? Use exact every 50 or if smooth lower.
      if exact lower (or every 100) update best
      if smooth_val? Could maintain best by exact every 20.
      if time > deadline break
   return best_h,best_c
```
Exact compute each iter with correlate O(n^2), but we already computed corr. We can compute exact C from same corr: `c = float(np.max(corr)) * dx`. No need second correlate. Good. Use c for best. `corr` already. So each iter one correlate and one gradient. Adam update. Good.

`compute_c5_from_h(h)`:
```
def compute_c5_from_h(h):
   h=np.asarray(h,float)
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
   corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```

def project_box_mean(y,n):
   y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
   target=0.5*n
   # quick uniform if feasible?
   lo = min(0 - y.min(), 1 - y.max())? Need τ range such that sum at τ=lo <=? x=clip(y+τ). τ low ->0, high ->1.
   lo = -max(y); hi = 1-min(y)

       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum()

```
def project_box_mean(y,n):
  x=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
  np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
  target=0.5*n
  for _ in range(20):
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    free=(x>0)&(x<1)
    cnt=free.sum()
    if cnt==0:
       # impossible unless target out of [0,n]; choose closest? set all 0.5? 
       x=target/n
       break
    x[free]+=diff/cnt
    np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
  diff=target-x.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     # distribute over least near-boundary
     # choose k = abs(diff) maybe ceil? assign to free
     free=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
     if free.any(): x[free]+=diff/free.sum(); np.clip...
     else:
        # if diff positive, add to x with smallest 1-x; else to largest x
        if diff>0:
           idx=np.argmin(1-x)
        else:
           idx=np.argmax(x)
        x[idx]=np.clip(x[idx]+diff,0,1)
  return x
```
This can fail if diff larger than one element capacity? But after iterations small. To be safe, final loop adjust multiple:
```
while abs(target-x.sum())>1e-14:
   free=(x>1e-14)&(x<1-1e-14)
   if not free.any(): break
   cnt=int(np.clip(abs(diff),1,free.size))
   # distribute diff to first cnt free by diff/cnt, but may exceed bounds; choose capacity-aware? simpler x[free]+=diff/free.size
```

```
def choose_target_binary_move(h,A,B,n,rng):
    # pick worst direction
    iA = int(np.argmax(A[1:])) + 1
    iB = int(np.argmax(B[1:])) + 1
    a = int(A[iA]); b=int(B[iB])
    if a >= b:
       L=iA; dirA=True
    else:
       L=iB; dirA=False
    if rng.random() < 0.2:
       L = rng.randint(1,n); dirA = (int(A[L]) >= int(B[L]))
    if dirA:
       if int(A[L]) > 0:
          valid = n-L
          for _ in range(30):
             i = rng.randint(valid)
             if h[i] == 0 and h[i+L] == 1:
                p1 = i+L; partner=i; break
       else fail
    else:
       valid=n-L
       for ... if h[i]==1 and h[i+L]==0: p1=i; partner=i+L
    if fail: return random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
    # p0
    for _ in range(30):
       p0=rng.randint(n)
       if h[p0]==0 and p0 != p1 and p0 != partner: break
    else:
       # fallback choose any zero except p1
       zeros = np.nonzero(h==0)[0]
       if len(zeros)==0: return None
       mask = zeros != p1
       if not mask.any(): mask[:] = True
       p0 = int(zeros[mask[rng.randint(mask.sum())]])
       if p0 == partner and len(zeros)>1:
          # maybe choose different from partner
          mask = (zeros != p1) & (zeros != partner)
          if mask.any(): p0=int(zeros[mask[rng.randint(mask.sum())]])
    return p1,p0
```
Calling `np.nonzero` rare.
Random pair function:
```
def random_binary_pair(h,n,rng):
   # choose one and zero
   for _ in range(20):
      p1=rng.randint(n)
      if h[p1]==1: break
   else: p1=int(np.nonzero(h==1)[0][0])
   for _ in range(20):
      p0=rng.randint(n)
      if h[p0]==0 and p0 != p1: break
   else: ...
   return p1,p0
```

Let's refine `binary_local_search` to not run indefinitely if `deadline` in past. In run, pass deadline absolute.

Pseudo code for helper:
```
def binary_local_search(h0, n, rng, deadline):
    n = int(n)//2*2
    if n < 4: n=4
    h = to_binary_values(h0, n)  # if h0 length same? uses binary threshold. For int h0, rounding same.
    A = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16); B=...
    A,B = init_counts(h,n)
    T,c1,c2,cost = eval_state(A,B)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_T=T; best_cost=cost
    temp=300.0; it=0; stall=0; pert_count=0
    while True:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       it += 1
       # move
       if rng.random() < 0.6:
          p1,p0 = choose_target_binary_move(h,A,B,n,rng)
       else:
          p1,p0 = random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
       if p1 is None: continue
       apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
       apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
       Tn,c1n,c2n,costn = eval_state(A,B)
       diff = costn - cost
       accept=False
       if diff <= -1e-9:
          accept=True
       elif diff < 1e-9:
          accept = (rng.random() < 0.35)
       else:
          # If diff small and temp high
          if diff < temp * 50: # to avoid underflow
             prob = math.exp(-diff/temp)
             accept = (rng.random() < prob)
          else:
             accept=False
       if accept:
          T,c1,c2,cost = Tn,c1n,c2n,costn
          if cost < best_cost - 1e-9 or (abs(cost-best_cost)<1e-9 and T < best_T):
             best_h = h.copy(); best_T=T; best_cost=cost
          if diff < -1e-9:
             stall=0
          else:
             stall += 1
       else:
          apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
          apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
          stall += 1
       if (it & 15) == 0:
          temp = max(1.0, temp * 0.9999)
       if stall > 4000:
          # perturbation
          accepted_pert=False
          limit = max(3, n//40)
          for __ in range(5):
             hsave=h.copy(); Asave=A.copy(); Bsave=B.copy()
             Tsave=T
             pertr= min(8, 2 + stall//4000)
             for k in range(pertr):
                pp1,pp0=random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
                if pp1 is None: break
                apply_flip(h,pp1,A,B,n); apply_flip(h,pp0,A,B,n)
             Tp,cp1,cp2,cp=eval_state(A,B)
             if Tp <= Tsave + limit or cp < Tsave*1000.0:
                # accept if not catastrophic; maybe if cp lower or T+limit
                if cp < cost or Tp <= Tsave + limit:
                   T,c1,c2,cost=Tp,cp1,cp2,cp
                   if cost < best_cost: best_h=h.copy(); best_T=T; best_cost=cost
                   accepted_pert=True
                   break
             else:
                h[:]=hsave; A[:]=Asave; B[:]=Bsave
          if not accepted_pert:
             # try reversal
             for __ in range(3):
                hsave=h.copy(); Asave=A.copy(); Bsave=B.copy(); Tsave=T; costsave=cost

Now `hierarchical_binary`: ensure length n and count n/2. Let's implement integer composition robustly.
```
def random_composition(total,k,rng):
   if total <=0: return np.zeros(k,dtype=np.int32)
   if k <=0: return np.array([total],dtype=np.int32)
   if k > total:
      # assign 1 to first total, rest 0
      arr=np.zeros(k,dtype=np.int32); arr[:total]=1; rng.shuffle(arr); return arr
   # Use Dirichlet with alpha to get positive-ish
   alpha = 0.6 + 0.8*rng.random()
   p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, alpha))
   counts = rng.multinomial(total, p).astype(np.int32)
   # if some zeros okay; but maybe want all positive if k<=total; add? Not necessary.
   # ensure sum total (multinomial does)
   return counts
```
But multinomial with p sums 1 returns sum total. Good. If k>total, can still use but zeros.

def hierarchical_binary(n,rng):
   target=n//2
   K = int(rng.choice([4,6,8,12,16,24]))
   a = random_composition(target,K,rng)
   b = random_composition(target,K,rng)
   h = np.empty(n,dtype=np.int16)
   pos=0
   for i in range(K):
      la=int(a[i]); lb=int(b[i])
      if la>0:
         if pos+la>n: la=n-pos
         h[pos:pos+la]=1; pos+=la

         h[pos:pos+lb]=0; pos+=lb

   # start sign random? complement; maybe run with zeros start sometimes.
   if rng.random()<0.5: h=1-h? But sum stays target. Complement valid.
   return h
```

`compute_c5_from_h(h)`:
```
def compute_c5_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)
```
If h has int, 1.0-h float.

`project_box_mean(y,n)`:
As above. Ensure if y size != n? Reshape? If size >n? Not expected. Could pad/truncate?
```
if y.size != n:
   # interpolate? call to float?
```
But used for continuous same n. Keep assert? If mismatch, return zeros. We can handle:
```

```
Not needed.
Implementation:
```
def project_box_mean(y, n):
    x = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    if x.size != n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n,float)
       m=min(x.size,n); tmp[:m]=x[:m]; x=tmp
    target = 0.5 * n
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    for _ in range(30):
       diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
           break
       free = (x > 1e-15) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-15)
       cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
       if cnt == 0:
           # set all to target/n within bounds
           x.fill(target / n)
           np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
           break
       x[free] += diff / cnt
       np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       for _ in range(20):
          free = (x > 1e-14) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-14)
          cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
          if cnt == 0: break
          # adjust only free by diff/cnt but limit to capacity? use small
          x[free] += diff / cnt
          np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
          diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
          if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
    # Final exact correction on one element if needed
    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff > 0:
          idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - x))
       else:
          idx = int(np.argmax(x))
       x[idx] = float(np.clip(x[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return x
```
This may not reach exact if correction exceeds capacity (diff > min slack). But previous loops should leave tiny diff. Could happen if all at bounds and target not exactly representable? target n/2, if n even, x all 0/1 sum integer; if sum off by >0.5, loops fail. But feasible. For continuous after Adam, uniform projection works.

`smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta)`:
```
def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    total = float(np.sum(exps))
    if total <= 0 or not np.isfinite(total):
        weights = np.zeros_like(corr); weights[np.argmax(corr)] = 1.0; return m, weights
    val = m + float(np.log(total)) / beta
    weights = exps / total
    return val, weights
```
`corr` float.

`continuous_gradient(weights,h,n)`:
Optimize by iterating only nonzero weights. We can skip `if w <= 1e-12`. Use local variables for speed. For `h` dtype float.
```
def continuous_gradient(weights, h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    # Instead of L loop negative to positive, maybe loop k index and lag
    for k in range(2*n - 1):
       w = float(weights[k])
       if w <= 1e-13: continue
       L = k - off
       if L == 0:
          grad += w * (1.0 - 2.0*h)
       elif L > 0:
          m = n - L
          grad[L:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
          grad[:m] -= w * h[L:]
       else:
          Lp = -L
          m = n - Lp
          grad[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[Lp:])
          grad[Lp:] -= w * h[:m]
    return dx * grad
```
The `h[:m]` view. For L negative, `h[Lp:]` length m. Good.

`slsqp_obj(x,beta)`:
```
def slsqp_obj(x, beta):
    n = len(x); dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    val, _ = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta)
    return float(val)*dx
```
SLSQP uses many evaluations; `smooth_max` uses exp. Good.
`slsqp_jac(x,beta)`:
```
def slsqp_jac(x,beta):
    n=len(x); dx=2/n
    corr=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    _, weights = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta)
    return continuous_gradient(weights,x,n)
```
`sum_constraint(x,n): return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)`

`slsqp_optimize(h0,n,beta,max_iter,deadline)`:
```
def slsqp_optimize(h0,n,beta,max_iter,deadline):
    h0 = project_box_mean(h0,n)
    # If time little, skip
    if time.time() >= deadline - 2: return h0, compute_c5_from_h(h0)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res = minimize(slsqp_obj, h0, method='SLSQP', jac=slsqp_jac, bounds=bounds,
                      constraints=cons, args=(beta,),
                      options={'maxiter': max_iter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'iprint':0})
       h = project_box_mean(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       h = h0
    c = compute_c5_from_h(h)
    return h,c
```
Need `from scipy.optimize import minimize` imported top.
Potential issue: `minimize` does not check deadline; but maxiter limited. If iter slow, could exceed. We can reduce maxiter based on n: in run compute. In function, could set `max_iter = max(10, min(max_iter, int(max(0, deadline - time.time()))//?))`? Not know per iter. Just limit externally.

`adam_optimize(h0,n,beta,max_iter,deadline,lr)`:
```
def adam_optimize(h0,n,beta,max_iter,deadline,lr=0.02):
   h=project_box_mean(h0,n)
   m=np.zeros(n,float); v=np.zeros(n,float); t=0
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5_from_h(h)
   beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.time() >= deadline - 2: break
      corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
      c=float(np.max(corr)*2.0/n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-15:
         best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
      _, weights = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta)
      g=continuous_gradient(weights,h,n)
      # if gradient zero maybe break
      t += 1
      m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
      v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*g*g
      mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
      vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
      h_new = h - lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
      h_new = project_box_mean(h_new,n)
      h = h_new
   c=compute_c5_from_h(h)
   if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
   return best_h,best_c
```
Compute exact c from corr before weights. Could skip if not. Good.
`beta1**t` maybe underflow; but t small. Use iterative bias? Fine. `1.0 - beta1 ** t` may be 1 for large.
lr may need scaled by n? For n=512, lr 0.02 okay. We'll set in run based on beta.

Now `to_binary_values(vals,n)`:
```
def to_binary_values(vals, n):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    if n <= 0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.int16)
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    if vals.size != n:
        src = vals.reshape(-1)
        n_src = src.size
        if n_src == 0:
            src = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            xsrc = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_src)
            xdst = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            src = np.interp(xdst, xsrc, src)
        vals = src
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
       return np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int16)
    if target >= n:
       return np.ones(n,dtype=np.int16)
    # If already exact binary balanced
    rounded = np.rint(vals)
    if np.all(np.abs(vals - rounded) < 1e-8):
        r = rounded.astype(np.int16)
        if int(np.sum(r)) == target:
            return r
    # ensure finite
    vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    idx = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:target]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    b[idx] = 1
    return b
```
For binary input with exact target 1s, returns same (because `args` maybe stable not needed). Great.
If parent has length n and binary balanced but dtype int with values 0/1, rounded sum target, returns copy. If length differs, interpolation fractional then threshold. Good.

`init_counts(h,n)`:
```
def init_counts(h,n):
    h32 = h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    corr = np.correlate(h32, 1 - h32, mode='full')
    A = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    if n > 1:
       A[1:] = corr[n:(2*n-1)].astype(np.int16)
       B[1:] = corr[n-2:0:-1].astype(np.int16)
    return A,B
```
Check `corr[n:(2*n-1)]`: length n-1 (indices n..2n-2 inclusive? Python stop 2n-1 exclusive -> n..2n-2 length n-1). For n=4, 4:7 indices 4,5,6. Good. `corr[n-2:0:-1]` for n=4 -> 2:0:-1 indices 2,1,0 length3. For n=2 -> n-2=0, stop0 step -1 length0, ok.
`1 - h32` int32.
If h int16, astype no copy? `copy=False` maybe. Good.

`eval_binary_state(A,B)`:
```
def eval_binary_state(A,B):
    if A.size <=1:
       return 0,0,0,0.0
    M = np.maximum(A[1:], B[1:])
    T = int(np.max(M))
    c1 = int(np.count_nonzero(M == T))
    if T > 0:
       c2 = int(np.count_nonzero(M >= T - 1))
    else:
       c2 = int(M.size)
    cost = 1000.0 * float(T) + 10.0 * float(c1) + float(c2)
    return T,c1,c2,cost
```
If M includes zeros, c2 includes all if T=0 (M>=-1). Fine.

`apply_flip(h,p,A,B,n)` as above. Use int16; when doing `B[1:m1+1] += 1 - h[...]`, temp int16. In-place add to int16 with temp int16 okay. Need ensure `1 - h[p+1...]` not overflow? h 0/1.
```
def apply_flip(h,p,A,B,n):
    old = int(h[p])
    if old == 0:
       m1 = n - p - 1
       if m1 > 0:
          A[1:m1+1] += h[p+1:p+n]
          B[1:m1+1] += 1 - h[p+1:p+n]
       m2 = p
       if m2 > 0:
          left = h[p-1::-1]
          A[1:m2+1] += 1 - left
          B[1:m2+1] -= left
       h[p] = 1
    else:
       m1 = n - p - 1
       if m1 > 0:
          A[1:m1+1] -= h[p+1:p+n]
          B[1:m1+1] -= 1 - h[p+1:p+n]
       m2 = p
       if m2 > 0:
          left = h[p-1::-1]
          A[1:m2+1] -= 1 - left
          B[1:m2+1] += left
       h[p] = 0
```
`B[1:m1+1] += 1 - h[...]` if h slice view int16, okay. `left` view reversed; `1-left` new.
Potential issue: `h[p+1:p+n]` length? p+n may >n, slice clamps to n; length n-p-? For p=0, p+n=n, length n; m1=n-1 but slice length n? Wait p+1=1, p+n=n, slice [1:n] length n-1? Since end n exclusive, length n-1. For p=0, m1=n-1. Good. For p=1, p+1=2,p+n=n+1 slice [2:n] length n-2=m1. Good.
`h[p-1::-1]` length p. Good.

`random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)`:
```
def random_binary_pair(h,n,rng):
   for _ in range(30):
      p1 = int(rng.randint(n))
      if h[p1] == 1:
         break
   else:
      ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
      if ones.size == 0: return None
      p1 = int(ones[0])
   for _ in range(30):
      p0 = int(rng.randint(n))
      if h[p0] == 0 and p0 != p1:
         break
   else:
      zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
      mask = zeros != p1
      if not np.any(mask): return None
      p0 = int(zeros[mask][int(rng.randint(int(np.count_nonzero(mask))))])
   return p1,p0
```
`np.count_nonzero(mask)` returns int. `rng.randint` expects Python int.
If no zero or one, None. But n balanced even, okay.

`choose_target_binary_move` as above. Need handle random lag and fail.
```
def choose_target_binary_move(h,A,B,n,rng):
    if n <= 1: return None
    iA = int(np.argmax(A[1:])) + 1
    iB = int(np.argmax(B[1:])) + 1
    a = int(A[iA]); b = int(B[iB])
    if a >= b:
       L = iA; dirA = True
    else:
       L = iB; dirA = False
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
       L = int(rng.randint(1, n))
       if L < 1: L=1
       if L > n-1: L=n-1
       dirA = (int(A[L]) >= int(B[L]))
    p1 = None; partner = -1
    valid = n - L
    if valid > 0:
       if dirA and int(A[L]) > 0:
          for _ in range(25):
             i = int(rng.randint(valid))
             if h[i] == 0 and h[i+L] == 1:
                p1 = i + L; partner = i; break
       elif (not dirA) and int(B[L]) > 0:
          for _ in range(25):
             i = int(rng.randint(valid))
             if h[i] == 1 and h[i+L] == 0:
                p1 = i; partner = i + L; break
    if p1 is None:
       return random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
    for _ in range(30):
       p0 = int(rng.randint(n))
       if h[p0] == 0 and p0 != p1 and p0 != partner:
          break
    else:
       zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
       mask = (zeros != p1) & (zeros != partner)
       if zeros.size == 0:
          return random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
       if not np.any(mask):
          mask = zeros != p1
       if not np.any(mask):
          return random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
       idx = int(rng.randint(int(np.count_nonzero(mask))))
       p0 = int(zeros[mask][idx])
    return p1,p0
```
`partner` may be 0; if `p0 != partner` and partner not zero? okay. If partner = -1? set -1, condition p0 != -1 always. Fine.
Potential bug: For A direction, partner=i could be 0. `p0 != partner` avoids swapping pair. Good.

def random_binary(n,rng):

    b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int16); target=n//2
    if target>0: b[rng.choice(n,target,replace=False)] = 1
    return b

Now `hierarchical_binary`: Need top-level random_composition. Let's implement robust.
```
def random_composition(total,k,rng):
    total=int(total); k=int(k)
    if total <= 0 or k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(max(k,1), dtype=np.int32) if k>0 else np.zeros(1,dtype=np.int32)
    if k == 1:
        return np.array([total], dtype=np.int32)
    if k > total:
        arr = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.int32)
        arr[:total] = 1
        rng.shuffle(arr)
        return arr
    alpha = 0.5 + 1.0 * float(rng.random())
    p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, alpha))
    counts = np.asarray(rng.multinomial(total, p), dtype=np.int32)
    # optional ensure no zeros? Could add one randomly to zeros? But sum fixed.
    return counts
```
If multinomial returns some zeros, fine; run length zero allowed.
`hierarchical_binary`:
```
def hierarchical_binary(n,rng):
    n=int(n)//2*2
    target=n//2
    K=int(rng.choice([4,6,8,12,16,24]))
    a=random_composition(target,K,rng)
    b=random_composition(target,K,rng)
    h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int16)
    pos=0
    # Random start sign? use start_one True/False
    start_one = (rng.random() < 0.5)
    # If start_one false, plus runs are zeros? Easier build plus ones, minus zeros, then complement if not start_one.
    for i in range(K):
       la=int(a[i]); lb=int(b[i])
       if start_one:
          if la>0 and pos<n:
             h[pos:pos+la]=1; pos+=la
          if lb>0 and pos<n:
             # zeros default
             pos+=lb
       else:
          if la>0 and pos<n:
             pos+=la  # zeros
          if lb>0 and pos<n:
             h[pos:pos+lb]=1; pos+=lb
    # Because total lengths n, pos==n. But if truncation? adjust count via threshold if needed.
    if int(np.sum(h)) != target:
       h = to_binary_values(h,n)
    return h
```
If start_one false, plus lengths represent zeros, minus ones; sum ones target (b sum) yes.
If la/lb > remaining? sums exact so no truncation; but safe.

`smooth_binary`:
```
def smooth_binary(n,rng):
   n=int(n)//2*2
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   f1=int(rng.randint(1,4)); f2=int(rng.randint(3,8)); f3=int(rng.randint(7,15))
   a1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.5)); a2=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.3)); a3=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2))
   p1=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); p2=...; p3=...
   vals=0.5 + a1*np.sin(2*np.pi*f1*x/n+p1) + a2*np.sin(2*np.pi*f2*x/n+p2) + a3*np.sin(2*np.pi*f3*x/n+p3)
   return to_binary_values(vals,n)
```

Need `run_binary_for_n` multi-start. Let's define top-level `binary_search_n(h_source,n,rng,deadline)` that does internal multi-start and deep. It can call `binary_local_search`. Top-level.
```
def binary_search_n(h_source,n,rng,deadline):
    n=int(n)//2*2
    source = h_source if h_source is not None else None
    parent_init = to_binary_values(source,n) if source is not None else hierarchical_binary(n,rng)
    best_h = parent_init
    best_c = 2.0 * init_counts(parent_init,n)[0].max()? Maybe compute via binary_local_search? Could evaluate quick.
    c = 2.0 / n * max((init_counts...)[0].max(), ...)
    best_by local? 
    # allocate fractions based on total time
    total = max(10, int(deadline - time.time()))
    if total < 20:
       return binary_local_search(parent_init,n,rng,deadline)
    frac_parent = 0.45 if total > 80 else 0.7
    # Create inits: parent, hier1, hier2, random maybe
    inits = [parent_init]
    if total > 60: inits.append(hierarchical_binary(n,rng))
    if total > 120: inits.append(hierarchical_binary(n,rng))
    if total > 180: inits.append(random_binary(n,rng))
    # assign times: first frac_parent, second 0.2, third 0.15, fourth 0.1, rest reserved
    times = []
    alloc = [frac_parent, 0.20, 0.15, 0.10]
    used=0
    for i,h0 in enumerate(inits):
       f = alloc[i] if i < len(alloc) else 0.05
       # ensure leave 0.1 for deep
       t = int(total * f)
       if i == len(inits)-1 or used+t > total*0.75: t = max(5, min(t, int(total*0.75)-used))? Simpler.
       times.append(max(10,t))
       used += times[-1]
    # run each until used <= 0.75 total
    for h0,t in zip(inits,times):
       if time.time() >= deadline - 20: break
       if t < 8: continue (or small)
       sec_dead = min(deadline - 20, time.time() + t)
       h,c = binary_local_search(h0,n,rng,sec_dead)
       if c < best_c: best_h, best_c = h,c
    # deep with remaining at least 10%
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h,c = binary_local_search(best_h,n,rng,deadline - 3)
       if c < best_c: best_h,best_c=h,c
    return best_h,best_c
```
This uses time fractions. If `binary_local_search` each from scratch, deep starts from best; okay. For small total, just parent.
But note: Initial `best_c` if we evaluate parent_init by `compute_c5_from_h`. For binary int, that's exact. We can use `compute_c5_from_h(best_h)` (float). Use for comparison. If continuous? parent_init int.
Need avoid evaluating parent_init before local; fine.

Let's think about `resample_real_vals` implemented top-level. It should handle if source is int binary length n; if size same, project. If source length diff, use np.interp of float values. Good.
```
def resample_real_vals(vals,n):
    vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=int(n)
    if n < 1: n=1
    if vals.size != n:
       if vals.size == 0: vals=np.full(n,0.5)
       else:
          xsrc=(np.arange(vals.size)+0.5)*(2.0/vals.size)
          xdst=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n))
          vals=np.interp(xdst,xsrc,vals)
    return project_box_mean(vals,n)
```
If n maybe odd, okay.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    hard_deadline = start + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None; best_c5 = float('inf'); best_n = 0
    parent_h = None
    try:
       parent_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if parent_h.size > 0:
          best_h = parent_h.copy()
          best_n = int(parent_h.size)
          best_c5 = compute_c5_from_h(best_h)
    except Exception:
       parent_h = None
    if best_h is None:
       best_n=200; best_h=np.zeros(best_n,float); best_h[:best_n//2]=1.0; best_c5=compute_c5_from_h(best_h)
    best_by_n = {}
    best_c_by_n = {}
    # store initial
    rn = int(best_n)
    if rn > 0:
       best_by_n[rn] = best_h.copy()
       best_c_by_n[rn] = best_c5
    base_n = rn if rn % 2 == 0 else 200
    if base_n < 96 or base_n > 400: base_n=200
    candidate_ns=[]
    for nn in [base_n,192,200,208,224,240,256,288,320,352,400]:
       nn=int(nn)//2*2
       if 96 <= nn <= 400 and nn not in candidate_ns:
          candidate_ns.append(nn)
    # order
    priorities = [base_n,256,200,224,240,288,320,208,192,352,400]
    ordered=[]
    for nn in priorities:
       if nn in candidate_ns and nn not in ordered: ordered.append(nn)
    for nn in candidate_ns:
       if nn not in ordered: ordered.append(nn)
    reserve_cont = min(320, max(120, int(0.25*budget_s)))
    reserve_final = 40
    max_bin = int(max(60, budget_s - reserve_cont - reserve_final))
    ns_fracs = [0.38,0.12,0.08,0.06,0.05,0.04,0.03,0.02,0.015,0.015,0.01,0.01]
    for idx,n in enumerate(ordered):
       if time.time() > hard_deadline - reserve_cont - reserve_final:
          break
       if idx < len(ns_fracs): frac=ns_fracs[idx]
       else: frac=0.005
       limit = int(max_bin * frac)
       if limit < 15: limit=15
       rem_ns = int(hard_deadline - time.time() - reserve_cont - reserve_final - 5)
       if rem_ns < 15: break
       if limit > rem_ns: limit = rem_ns
       n_deadline = time.time() + limit
       source = best_by_n.get(n, best_h)
       h_b, c_b = binary_search_n(source, n, rng, n_deadline)
       if h_b is not None and c_b < float('inf'):
          if n not in best_c_by_n or c_b < best_c_by_n[n]:
             best_by_n[n] = h_b.copy()
             best_c_by_n[n] = c_b
          if c_b < best_c5:
             best_c5 = c_b; best_h = h_b.copy(); best_n = n
    # deep best
    rem = int(hard_deadline - time.time() - 30)
    if rem > 60:
       n = best_n
       if n % 2 != 0:
          # keep continuous? binary not good. Could use even resampling best_n
          n = int(n)//2*2
       if n < 96: n = 200
       source = best_by_n.get(n, None)
       if source is None: source = best_h
       deep_limit = min(int(rem*0.8), 400)
       h_b,c_b = binary_search_n(source, n, rng, time.time()+deep_limit)
       update...
    # second deep if time
    rem = int(hard_deadline - time.time() - 40 - 120?) Leave continuous.

Detailed functions:

`smooth_max_value`:
```
def smooth_max_value(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    total = float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    if total <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(total):
        return m
    return m + math.log(total) / beta
```
`math.isfinite` top import. Good.

```
def binary_local_search(h0, n, rng, deadline):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    if n <= 0:
       return h0, float('inf')
    h = to_binary_values(h0, n)
    A, B = init_counts(h, n)
    T, c1, c2, cost = eval_binary_state(A, B)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cost = cost
    temp = 300.0
    min_temp = 1.0
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    while True:
       if time.time() >= deadline:
          break
       it += 1
       if it % 64 == 0 and time.time() >= deadline:
          break
       if rng.random() < 0.60:
          p1,p0 = choose_target_binary_move(h,A,B,n,rng)
       else:
          p1,p0 = random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
       if p1 is None: continue
       apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
       apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
       Tn,c1n,c2n,costn = eval_binary_state(A,B)
       diff = costn - cost
       accept = False
       if diff <= -1e-9:
          accept = True
       elif diff < 1e-9:
          accept = (rng.random() < 0.35)
       else:
          if diff < temp * 700.0:
             prob = math.exp(-diff/temp)
             accept = (rng.random() < prob)
       if accept:
          T,c1,c2,cost = Tn,c1n,c2n,costn
          if cost < best_cost:
             best_cost = cost
             best_h = h.copy()
          if diff < -1e-9:
             stall = 0
          else:
             stall += 1
       else:
          apply_flip(h,p1,A,B,n)
          apply_flip(h,p0,A,B,n)
          stall += 1
       if (it & 15) == 0:
          temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.9998)
       if stall > 4000:
          accepted_pert = False
          limit = max(3, n // 40)
          for __ in range(5):
             hsave = h.copy(); Asave = A.copy(); Bsave = B.copy()
             Tsave = T; c1save=c1; c2save=c2; costsave=cost
             pert = min(10, 2 + stall // 4000)
             for k in range(pert):
                pp1,pp0 = random_binary_pair(h,n,rng)
                if pp1 is None: break
                apply_flip(h,pp1,A,B,n)
                apply_flip(h,pp0,A,B,n)
             Tp,c1p,c2p,cp = eval_binary_state(A,B)
             if cp < costsave or Tp <= Tsave + limit:
                T,c1,c2,cost = Tp,c1p,c2p,cp
                if cost < best_cost:
                   best_cost=cost; best_h=h.copy()
                accepted_pert=True
                break
             else:
                h[:] = hsave; A[:] = Asave; B[:] = Bsave
                T,c1,c2,cost = Tsave,c1save,c2save,costsave
          if not accepted_pert:
             # reversal
             for __ in range(3):
                hsave=h.copy(); Asave=A.copy(); Bsave=B.copy()
                Tsave=T; c1save=c1; c2save=c2; costsave=cost
                if n >= 8:
                   l = int(rng.randint(0, n-1))
                   r = int(rng.randint(l+1, n))
                   h[l:r+1] = h[l:r+1][::-1]
                   An, Bn = init_counts(h, n)
                   Tn2,c1n2,c2n2,cn2 = eval_binary_state(An,Bn)
                   if cn2 < costsave or Tn2 <= Tsave + limit:
                      A,B = An,Bn
                      T,c1,c2,cost = Tn2,c1n2,c2n2,cn2
                      if cost < best_cost:
                         best_cost=cost; best_h=h.copy()
                      accepted_pert=True
                      break
                if not accepted_pert:
                   h[:] = hsave; A[:] = Asave; B[:] = Bsave
                   T,c1,c2,cost = Tsave,c1save,c2save,costsave
          stall = 0
          temp = max(temp, 120.0)
          if time.time() >= deadline:
             break
```
Potential subtle bug: In reversal branch, if accepted and break, `accepted_pert` true. If n<8, `accepted_pert` remains false after for loop? It will go next reversal with same? But n<8 not used. okay.
If in reversal branch after first attempt accepted, break; after loop `stall=0`. Good.

```
def binary_search_n(h_source,n,rng,deadline):
   n=int(n)//2*2
   if n < 2: n=2
   if h_source is None:
      init = hierarchical_binary(n,rng)
   else:
      init = to_binary_values(h_source,n)
   total = int(deadline - time.time())
   if total <= 10:
      return init, compute_c5_from_h(init)
   best_h = init
   best_c = compute_c5_from_h(best_h)
   inits=[best_h]
   if total > 60:
      inits.append(hierarchical_binary(n,rng))
   if total > 160:
      inits.append(random_binary(n,rng))
   if len(inits) == 1:
      h,c = binary_local_search(best_h,n,rng,deadline)
      return h,c
   fracs=[0.45,0.30,0.15,0.10][:len(inits)]
   ssum=0.0
   for f in fracs: ssum += f
   scale = 0.62 / ssum if ssum>0 else 0.0
   for i in range(len(inits)):
      if time.time() >= deadline - 20: break
      t = int(total * fracs[i] * scale)
      if t < 5: t = 5
      # not exceed deadline-20
      sec_dead = time.time() + t
      if sec_dead > deadline - 20: sec_dead = deadline - 20
      if sec_dead <= time.time(): continue
      h,c = binary_local_search(inits[i], n, rng, sec_dead)
      if h is not None and c < best_c:
         best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c
   if time.time() < deadline - 15:
      h,c = binary_local_search(best_h,n,rng,deadline - 3)
      if h is not None and c < best_c:
         best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c
   return best_h, float(best_c)
```
Potential issue: `best_h` initial int16; after quick, `h` from binary_local_search int16; `best_c` float. Good. If total small, return initial c. If total <=10, local not run, perhaps not optimal but brief.
If `h_source` is continuous fractional length n, `to_binary_values` threshold. Good.

diff = target - x.sum()
if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
    for _ in range(10):
       free = (x > 0) & (x < 1)
       cnt = free.sum()
       if cnt == 0: break
       x[free] += diff / cnt
       np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
       diff = target - x.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
# if still, adjust one with max capacity
diff = target - x.sum()
if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
   if diff > 0:
      idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - x))
   else:
      idx = int(np.argmax(x))
   newval = x[idx] + diff
   if newval < 0 or newval > 1:
      # fallback: redistribute to all by small?
      x[:] = np.clip(x + diff / n, 0, 1)
   else:
      x[idx] = newval
```

For Adam, 1000 iterations * 50 * n? 25M, fine. For n=500, 50*500=25k per iteration, 1000=25M, okay. For SLSQP final projection, fine. Let's implement more robust exact water fill:
```
def project_box_mean(y,n):
    y = np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    if y.size != n: pad/trunc
    target = 0.5*n
    # find tau by bisection
    lo = -float(np.max(y))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(y))
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        x = np.clip(y + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(y + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    # adjust tiny by free elements
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       free=(x>1e-14)&(x<1-1e-14)
       cnt=free.sum()
       if cnt>0:
           x[free] += diff/cnt
           np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
       # if still, binary search maybe not exact due flat? but should
       diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
       if abs(diff)>1e-12:
          # final distribute among all with capacity ignoring tiny: use y? simpler adjust one
          ...
    return x
```
Bisection 60 iterations maybe overkill; 45 enough. Sum monotone piecewise linear. If y has huge values, lo/hi cover. E.g. y all 0.5, lo=-0.5 hi=0.5, mid0 sum n/2 exactly. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def project_box_mean(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        m = min(y.size, n)
        tmp[:m] = y[:m]
        y = tmp
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0).copy()
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    lo = -float(np.max(y))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(y))
    x = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        x = y + mid
        np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    x = y + tau
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        free = (x > 1e-15) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-15)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
        if cnt > 0:
            x[free] += diff / float(cnt)
            np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        diff = target - float(np.sum(x))

    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        for _ in range(20):
            if diff > 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - x))
                cap = float(1.0 - x[idx])
                add = min(cap, diff)
                if add <= 1e-18:
                    break
                x[idx] += add
                diff -= add
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(x))
                cap = float(x[idx])
                sub = min(cap, -diff)
                if sub <= 1e-18:
                    break
                x[idx] -= sub
                diff += sub
            if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                break
    return x


def smooth_max_value(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    total = float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    if total <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(total):
        return m
    return m + math.log(total) / beta


def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    total = float(np.sum(exps))
    if total <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(total):
        weights = np.zeros_like(corr, dtype=float)
        weights[int(np.argmax(corr))] = 1.0
        return m, weights
    val = m + math.log(total) / beta
    weights = exps / total
    return val, weights


def continuous_gradient(weights, h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        w = float(weights[k])
        if w <= 1e-13:
            continue
        L = k - off
        if L == 0:
            grad += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif L > 0:
            m = n - L
            grad[L:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
            grad[:m] -= w * h[L:]
        else:
            Lp = -L
            m = n - Lp
            grad[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[Lp:])
            grad[Lp:] -= w * h[:m]
    return dx * grad


def sum_constraint(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(int(n)))


def slsqp_obj(x, beta):
    n = int(len(x))
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    return float(smooth_max_value(corr, beta) * dx)


def slsqp_jac(x, beta):
    n = int(len(x))
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    _, weights = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta)
    return continuous_gradient(weights, x, n)


def slsqp_optimize(h0, n, beta, max_iter, deadline):
    h0 = project_box_mean(h0, int(n))
    if int(max_iter) <= 0 or time.time() >= float(deadline) - 2.0:
        return h0, compute_c5_from_h(h0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "args": (int(n),)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_obj,
            h0,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=slsqp_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            args=(float(beta),),
            options={"maxiter": int(max_iter), "ftol": 1e-12, "iprint": 0},
        )
        if res.x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            h = h0
        else:
            h = project_box_mean(res.x, int(n))
    except Exception:
        h = h0
    c = compute_c5_from_h(h)
    return h, float(c)


def adam_optimize(h0, n, beta, max_iter, deadline, lr):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_mean(h0, n)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    bias1 = 1.0
    bias2 = 1.0
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_from_h(h)

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 2.0:
            break
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        c = float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / float(n)))
        if c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()

        _, weights = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
        g = continuous_gradient(weights, h, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        bias1 *= beta1
        bias2 *= beta2
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        m_hat = m / (1.0 - bias1)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - bias2)

        h_new = h - float(lr) * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
        h = project_box_mean(h_new, n)

    c = compute_c5_from_h(h)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def to_binary_values(vals, n):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int16)
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if vals.size != n:
        n_src = int(vals.size)
        if n_src == 0:
            vals = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        else:
            x_src = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_src))
            x_dst = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            vals = np.interp(x_dst, x_src, vals)

    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.int16)

    rounded = np.rint(vals)
    if np.all(np.abs(vals - rounded) < 1e-8):
        r = rounded.astype(np.int16)
        if int(np.sum(r)) == target:
            return r

    vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    idx = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:target]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    b[idx] = 1
    return b


def resample_real_vals(vals, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if vals.size != n:
        n_src = int(vals.size)
        if n_src == 0:
            vals = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        else:
            x_src = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_src))
            x_dst = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            vals = np.interp(x_dst, x_src, vals)
    return project_box_mean(vals, n)


def init_counts(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h32 = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int64).astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    corr = np.correlate(h32, 1 - h32, mode="full")
    A = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    if n > 1:
        A[1:] = corr[n:(2 * n - 1)].astype(np.int16)
        B[1:] = corr[n - 2::-1].astype(np.int16)
    return A, B


def eval_binary_state(A, B):
    if A.size <= 1:
        return 0, 0, 0, 0.0
    M = np.maximum(A[1:], B[1:])
    T = int(np.max(M))
    c1 = int(np.count_nonzero(M == T))
    if T > 0:
        c2 = int(np.count_nonzero(M >= T - 1))
    else:
        c2 = int(M.size)
    cost = 1000.0 * float(T) + 10.0 * float(c1) + float(c2)
    return T, c1, c2, cost


def apply_flip(h, p, A, B, n):
    n = int(n)
    old = int(h[p])
    if old == 0:
        m1 = n - p - 1
        if m1 > 0:
            A[1:m1 + 1] += h[p + 1:p + n]
            B[1:m1 + 1] += 1 - h[p + 1:p + n]
        m2 = p
        if m2 > 0:
            left = h[p - 1::-1]
            A[1:m2 + 1] += 1 - left
            B[1:m2 + 1] -= left
        h[p] = 1
    else:
        m1 = n - p - 1
        if m1 > 0:
            A[1:m1 + 1] -= h[p + 1:p + n]
            B[1:m1 + 1] -= 1 - h[p + 1:p + n]
        m2 = p
        if m2 > 0:
            left = h[p - 1::-1]
            A[1:m2 + 1] -= 1 - left
            B[1:m2 + 1] += left
        h[p] = 0


def random_binary_pair(h, n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    for _ in range(30):
        p1 = int(rng.randint(n))
        if h[p1] == 1:
            break
    else:
        ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
        if ones.size == 0:
            return None
        p1 = int(ones[0])

    for _ in range(30):
        p0 = int(rng.randint(n))
        if h[p0] == 0 and p0 != p1:
            break
    else:
        zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
        mask = zeros != p1
        if zeros.size == 0 or not np.any(mask):
            return None
        count = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        p0 = int(zeros[mask][int(rng.randint(count))])
    return p1, p0


def choose_target_binary_move(h, A, B, n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return None

    iA = int(np.argmax(A[1:])) + 1
    iB = int(np.argmax(B[1:])) + 1
    a = int(A[iA])
    b = int(B[iB])
    if a >= b:
        L = iA
        dirA = True
    else:
        L = iB
        dirA = False

    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        L = int(rng.randint(1, n))
        if L < 1:
            L = 1
        if L > n - 1:
            L = n - 1
        dirA = (int(A[L]) >= int(B[L]))

    p1 = None
    partner = -1
    valid = n - L
    if valid > 0:
        if dirA and int(A[L]) > 0:
            for _ in range(25):
                i = int(rng.randint(valid))
                if h[i] == 0 and h[i + L] == 1:
                    p1 = i + L
                    partner = i
                    break
        elif (not dirA) and int(B[L]) > 0:
            for _ in range(25):
                i = int(rng.randint(valid))
                if h[i] == 1 and h[i + L] == 0:
                    p1 = i
                    partner = i + L
                    break

    if p1 is None:
        return random_binary_pair(h, n, rng)

    for _ in range(30):
        p0 = int(rng.randint(n))
        if h[p0] == 0 and p0 != p1 and p0 != partner:
            break
    else:
        zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
        if zeros.size == 0:
            return random_binary_pair(h, n, rng)
        mask = (zeros != p1) & (zeros != partner)
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = zeros != p1
        if not np.any(mask):
            return random_binary_pair(h, n, rng)
        count = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        p0 = int(zeros[mask][int(rng.randint(count))])

    return p1, p0


def random_binary(n, rng):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    target = n // 2
    if target > 0:
        b[rng.choice(n, target, replace=False)] = 1
    return b


def smooth_binary(n, rng):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    f1 = int(rng.randint(1, 4))
    f2 = int(rng.randint(3, 8))
    f3 = int(rng.randint(7, 15))
    a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.5))
    a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.3))
    a3 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    p3 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    vals = (
        0.5
        + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x / float(n) + p1)
        + a2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x / float(n) + p2)
        + a3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f3 * x / float(n) + p3)
    )
    return to_binary_values(vals, n)


def random_composition(total, k, rng):
    total = int(total)
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.int32)
    if total <= 0:
        return np.zeros(k, dtype=np.int32)
    if k == 1:
        return np.array([total], dtype=np.int32)
    if k > total:
        arr = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.int32)
        arr[:total] = 1
        rng.shuffle(arr)
        return arr
    alpha = 0.5 + 1.0 * float(rng.random())
    p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, alpha))
    counts = np.asarray(rng.multinomial(total, p), dtype=np.int32)
    return counts


def hierarchical_binary(n, rng):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    target = n // 2
    K = int(rng.choice([4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24]))
    a = random_composition(target, K, rng)
    b = random_composition(target, K, rng)
    start_one = (rng.random() < 0.5)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int16)
    pos = 0
    for i in range(K):
        la = int(a[i])
        lb = int(b[i])
        if start_one:
            if la > 0 and pos < n:
                take = min(la, n - pos)
                h[pos:pos + take] = 1
                pos += take
            if lb > 0 and pos < n:
                take = min(lb, n - pos)
                pos += take
        else:
            if la > 0 and pos < n:
                take = min(la, n - pos)
                pos += take
            if lb > 0 and pos < n:
                take = min(lb, n - pos)
                h[pos:pos + take] = 1
                pos += take
    if int(np.sum(h)) != target:
        h = to_binary_values(h, n)
    return h


def binary_local_search(h0, n, rng, deadline):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int16), float("inf")

    h = to_binary_values(h0, n)
    A, B = init_counts(h, n)
    T, c1, c2, cost = eval_binary_state(A, B)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cost = cost

    temp = 300.0
    min_temp = 1.0
    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while True:
        if time.time() >= float(deadline):
            break
        it += 1
        if (it & 63) == 0 and time.time() >= float(deadline):
            break

        if rng.random() < 0.60:
            p1, p0 = choose_target_binary_move(h, A, B, n, rng)
        else:
            p1, p0 = random_binary_pair(h, n, rng)
        if p1 is None:
            continue

        apply_flip(h, p1, A, B, n)
        apply_flip(h, p0, A, B, n)
        Tn, c1n, c2n, costn = eval_binary_state(A, B)
        diff = costn - cost

        accept = False
        if diff <= -1e-9:
            accept = True
        elif diff < 1e-9:
            accept = (rng.random() < 0.35)
        else:
            if diff < temp * 700.0:
                prob = math.exp(-diff / temp)
                accept = (rng.random() < prob)

        if accept:
            T, c1, c2, cost = Tn, c1n, c2n, costn
            if cost < best_cost:
                best_cost = cost
                best_h = h.copy()
            if diff < -1e-9:
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            apply_flip(h, p1, A, B, n)
            apply_flip(h, p0, A, B, n)
            stall += 1

        if (it & 15) == 0:
            temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.9998)

        if stall > 4000:
            accepted_pert = False
            limit = max(3, n // 40)

            for __ in range(5):
                hsave = h.copy()
                Asave = A.copy()
                Bsave = B.copy()
                Tsave = T
                c1save = c1
                c2save = c2
                costsave = cost

                pert = min(10, 2 + stall // 4000)
                for _ in range(pert):
                    pp1, pp0 = random_binary_pair(h, n, rng)
                    if pp1 is None:
                        break
                    apply_flip(h, pp1, A, B, n)
                    apply_flip(h, pp0, A, B, n)

                Tp, c1p, c2p, cp = eval_binary_state(A, B)
                if cp < costsave or Tp <= Tsave + limit:
                    T, c1, c2, cost = Tp, c1p, c2p, cp
                    if cost < best_cost:
                        best_cost = cost
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    accepted_pert = True
                    break
                else:
                    h[:] = hsave
                    A[:] = Asave
                    B[:] = Bsave
                    T, c1, c2, cost = Tsave, c1save, c2save, costsave

            if not accepted_pert:
                for __ in range(3):
                    hsave = h.copy()
                    Asave = A.copy()
                    Bsave = B.copy()
                    Tsave = T
                    c1save = c1
                    c2save = c2
                    costsave = cost
                    if n >= 8:
                        l = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
                        r = int(rng.randint(l + 1, n))
                        h[l:r + 1] = h[l:r + 1][::-1]
                        An, Bn = init_counts(h, n)
                        Tn2, c1n2, c2n2, cn2 = eval_binary_state(An, Bn)
                        if cn2 < costsave or Tn2 <= Tsave + limit:
                            A, B = An, Bn
                            T, c1, c2, cost = Tn2, c1n2, c2n2, cn2
                            if cost < best_cost:
                                best_cost = cost
                                best_h = h.copy()
                            accepted_pert = True
                            break
                    if not accepted_pert:
                        h[:] = hsave
                        A[:] = Asave
                        B[:] = Bsave
                        T, c1, c2, cost = Tsave, c1save, c2save, costsave

            stall = 0
            temp = max(temp, 120.0)
            if time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break

    try:
        best_c = compute_c5_from_h(best_h.astype(float))
    except Exception:
        best_c = 2.0 * float(T) / float(n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def binary_search_n(h_source, n, rng, deadline):
    n = int(n) // 2 * 2
    if n < 2:
        n = 2

    if h_source is None:
        init = hierarchical_binary(n, rng)
    else:
        init = to_binary_values(h_source, n)

    total = int(float(deadline) - time.time())
    if total <= 10:
        return init, compute_c5_from_h(init)

    best_h = init
    best_c = compute_c5_from_h(best_h)

    inits = [best_h]
    if total > 60:
        inits.append(hierarchical_binary(n, rng))
    if total > 160:
        inits.append(random_binary(n, rng))

    if len(inits) == 1:
        h2, c2 = binary_local_search(best_h, n, rng, deadline)
        return h2, float(c2)

    fracs = [0.45, 0.30, 0.15, 0.10][:len(inits)]
    ssum = 0.0
    for f in fracs:
        ssum += f
    scale = 0.62 / ssum if ssum > 0.0 else 0.0

    for i in range(len(inits)):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 20.0:
            break
        t = int(total * fracs[i] * scale)
        if t < 5:
            t = 5
        sec_dead = time.time() + float(t)
        if sec_dead > float(deadline) - 20.0:
            sec_dead = float(deadline) - 20.0
        if sec_dead <= time.time():
            continue
        hh, cc = binary_local_search(inits[i], n, rng, sec_dead)
        if hh is not None and float(cc) < best_c:
            best_h = hh.copy()
            best_c = float(cc)

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 15.0:
        hh, cc = binary_local_search(best_h, n, rng, float(deadline) - 3.0)
        if hh is not None and float(cc) < best_c:
            best_h = hh.copy()
            best_c = float(cc)

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    alpha = float(budget_s)
    if seed is None:
        seed = 42
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0
    parent_h = None

    try:
        parent_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if parent_h.size > 0:
            best_h = parent_h.copy()
            best_n = int(parent_h.size)
            best_c5 = compute_c5_from_h(best_h)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = np.zeros(best_n, dtype=float)
        best_h[:best_n // 2] = 1.0
        best_c5 = compute_c5_from_h(best_h)

    best_by_n = {}
    best_c_by_n = {}
    if best_n > 0:
        best_by_n[best_n] = best_h.copy()
        best_c_by_n[best_n] = best_c5

    base_n = int(best_n)
    if base_n % 2 != 0:
        base_n = 200
    if base_n < 96 or base_n > 400:
        base_n = 200

    candidate_ns = []
    for nn in [base_n, 192, 200, 208, 224, 240, 256, 288, 320, 352, 400]:
        nn = int(nn) // 2 * 2
        if 96 <= nn <= 400 and nn not in candidate_ns:
            candidate_ns.append(nn)

    priorities = [base_n, 256, 200, 224, 240, 288, 320, 208, 192, 352, 400]
    ordered = []
    for nn in priorities:
        if nn in candidate_ns and nn not in ordered:
            ordered.append(nn)
    for nn in candidate_ns:
        if nn not in ordered:
            ordered.append(nn)

    if alpha > 600.0:
        reserve_cont = int(min(300.0, max(120.0, 0.22 * alpha)))
    else:
        reserve_cont = int(max(10.0, 0.30 * alpha))
    reserve_final = int(min(20.0, max(3.0, 0.05 * alpha)))
    total_for_binary = int(alpha - reserve_cont - reserve_final)
    if total_for_binary < 30:
        reserve_cont = 0
        reserve_final = 0
        total_for_binary = max(10, int(alpha) - 5)

    binary_deadline = start_time + float(total_for_binary)
    hard_deadline = start_time + alpha - 3.0

    ns_fracs = [0.32, 0.16, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05, 0.04, 0.03, 0.02, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01]
    for idx, nn in enumerate(ordered):
        if time.time() > binary_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if idx < len(ns_fracs):
            frac = float(ns_fracs[idx])
        else:
            frac = 0.005
        limit = int(float(total_for_binary) * frac)
        if limit < 15:
            limit = 15
        rem_ns = int(binary_deadline - time.time() - 5.0)
        if rem_ns < 15:
            break
        if limit > rem_ns:
            limit = rem_ns
        n_deadline = time.time() + float(limit)
        source = best_by_n.get(nn, best_h)
        h_b, c_b = binary_search_n(source, nn, rng, n_deadline)
        if h_b is not None and math.isfinite(float(c_b)):
            if nn not in best_c_by_n or float(c_b) < best_c_by_n[nn]:
                best_by_n[nn] = h_b.copy()
                best_c_by_n[nn] = float(c_b)
            if float(c_b) < best_c5:
                best_c5 = float(c_b)
                best_h = h_b.copy()
                best_n = nn

    rem_bin = int(binary_deadline - time.time() - 5.0)
    if rem_bin > 40:
        nn = int(best_n)
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            nn = nn // 2 * 2
        if nn < 96:
            nn = 200
        source = best_by_n.get(nn, best_h)
        deep_limit = min(int(float(rem_bin) * 0.9), 300)
        h_b, c_b = binary_search_n(source, nn, rng, time.time() + float(deep_limit))
        if h_b is not None and math.isfinite(float(c_b)):
            if nn not in best_c_by_n or float(c_b) < best_c_by_n[nn]:
                best_by_n[nn] = h_b.copy()
                best_c_by_n[nn] = float(c_b)
            if float(c_b) < best_c5:
                best_c5 = float(c_b)
                best_h = h_b.copy()
                best_n = nn

    cont_cands = []
    seen = []
    if best_h is not None and best_n > 0:
        cn = int(best_n)
        cont_cands.append((cn, best_h))
        seen.append(cn)
    if parent_h is not None and parent_h.size > 0:
        pn = int(parent_h.size)
        if pn not in seen:
            cont_cands.append((pn, parent_h))
            seen.append(pn)
    for nn in ordered:
        if nn in best_c_by_n and nn not in seen and len(cont_cands) < 6:
            cont_cands.append((nn, best_by_n[nn]))
            seen.append(nn)
    if len(cont_cands) > 4:
        cont_cands = cont_cands[:4]

    cont_deadline = hard_deadline - 5.0
    rem_cont = int(cont_deadline - time.time() - 2.0)
    if rem_cont > 20 and len(cont_cands) > 0:
        per = max(20, int(float(rem_cont) / float(max(1, len(cont_cands)))))
        for nn, h0 in cont_cands:
            if time.time() >= cont_deadline - 5.0:
                break
            rem = int(cont_deadline - time.time() - 2.0)
            if rem < 20:
                break
            cand_budget = min(180, per, rem)
            if cand_budget < 15:
                break
            cand_deadline = time.time() + float(cand_budget)

            nn = int(nn) if int(nn) > 1 else max(2, int(best_n))
            h_float = resample_real_vals(h0, nn)

            t1 = min(60, int((cand_deadline - time.time()) * 0.35))
            if t1 > 8:
                h2, c2 = adam_optimize(h_float, nn, 10.0, 1000000, time.time() + float(t1), 0.03)
                if float(c2) < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = float(c2)
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    best_n = nn

            if time.time() >= cand_deadline - 8.0:
                continue
            t2 = min(40, int((cand_deadline - time.time()) * 0.30))
            if t2 > 8:
                h3, c3 = adam_optimize(h2, nn, 60.0, 1000000, time.time() + float(t2), 0.012)
                if float(c3) < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = float(c3)
                    best_h = h3.copy()
                    best_n = nn
            else:
                h3 = h2

            if time.time() >= cand_deadline - 10.0:
                continue
            rem = int(cand_deadline - time.time() - 2.0)
            if rem > 12:
                if nn <= 256:
                    miter = 35
                elif nn <= 320:
                    miter = 25
                else:
                    miter = 18
                t3 = min(60, int((cand_deadline - time.time()) * 0.40))
                h4, c4 = slsqp_optimize(h3, nn, 120.0, miter, time.time() + float(t3))
                if float(c4) < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = float(c4)
                    best_h = h4.copy()
                    best_n = nn

                if time.time() < cand_deadline - 10.0:
                    miter2 = max(10, miter // 2)
                    h5, c5 = slsqp_optimize(h4, nn, 400.0, miter2, cand_deadline - 2.0)
                    if float(c5) < best_c5:
                        best_c5 = float(c5)
                        best_h = h5.copy()
                        best_n = nn

    rem = int(hard_deadline - time.time() - 5.0)
    if rem > 60 and int(best_n) % 2 == 0:
        b = to_binary_values(best_h, int(best_n))
        h_b, c_b = binary_local_search(b, int(best_n), rng, hard_deadline - 5.0)
        if h_b is not None and math.isfinite(float(c_b)) and float(c_b) < best_c5:
            best_c5 = float(c_b)
            best_h = h_b.copy()

    best_n = int(best_n)
    if best_n < 1 or best_h is None:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = np.zeros(best_n, dtype=float)
        best_h[:best_n // 2] = 1.0

    final_h = project_box_mean(best_h, best_n)
    final_c5 = compute_c5_from_h(final_h)

    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(final_h)
        if isinstance(ec, (tuple, list)):
            ec = ec[0]
        ec = float(ec)
        if math.isfinite(ec) and ec < final_c5:
            final_c5 = ec
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n)
```
